$('.ws-grid-container a').click(function () {
    // collect button text
    wsBtnText = $(this).find('.ws-btn-autumn').text().trim().replace(/'/g, '');

    // console log area clicked

        'eventlabel' : 'HP_AW-FY20'  
    });

        speed: 400,
        autoplay: false,

        dots: true,
        responsive: [
            {
                breakpoint: 991,
                settings: {
                    centerMode: true,
                    centerPadding: '0',
                    slidesToShow: 2,
                    slidesToScroll: 2,
                }
            },
            {
                breakpoint: 650,
                settings: {
                    centerMode: false,
                    slidesToShow: 1,
                    slidesToScroll: 1,
                }
            }
        ]
    });

    // "View More" - Gather and push click data
    $('.ws-content--scproductslider .ws-btn').click(function () {
        // collect product sku
        wsSliderProductSku = $(this).parent().find('.product-tile__quickview-trigger').attr('data-product-sku');

        // collect product name
        wsSliderProductName = $(this).parent().parent().find('h3').text();

        // console log product sku and name
        console.log("button click product code: " + wsSliderProductSku);
        console.log("button click product name: " + wsSliderProductName);

        // push view more data to dataLayer
        dataLayer.push({
            'event': 'hp_product_carousel',
            'eventaction' : 'View More clicked', 
            'eventcategory' : 'HP Product Carousel',
            'eventlabel' : wsSliderProductSku + " " + wsSliderProductName  
        });        
    });


    // "Quick View" - Gather and push click data
    $('.ws-content--scproductslider .product-tile__quickview-trigger').click(function () {
        // collect product sku
        wsSliderProductSku = $(this).parent().find('.product-tile__quickview-trigger').attr('data-product-sku');

        // collect product name
        wsSliderProductName = $(this).parent().parent().find('h3').text();

        // console log product sku and name
        console.log("quickview click product code: " + wsSliderProductSku);
        console.log("quickview click product name: " + wsSliderProductName);

        // push quick view data to dataLayer
        dataLayer.push({
            'event': 'hp_product_carousel',
            'eventaction' : 'Quick View clicked', 
            'eventcategory' : 'HP Product Carousel',
            'eventlabel' : wsSliderProductSku + " " + wsSliderProductName  
        });   
    });

    // Pagination click - Gather and push click data
    $('.slick-arrow').click(function () {
        // track click on pagination arrow (left or right)
        wsSliderArrow = $(this).attr('aria-label');

        // console.log pagination arrow type
        console.log("Product Scroller Arrow " + wsSliderArrow);

        // push pagination data to dataLayer
        dataLayer.push({
            'event': 'hp_product_carousel',
            'eventaction' : 'Pagination clicked', 
            'eventcategory' : 'HP Product Carousel',
            'eventlabel' : "Product Scroller Arrow " + wsSliderArrow  
        }); 
    });

    // Product image click - Gather and push click data
    $('.ws-content--scproductslider .ws-slide__quickviewcontainer img').click(function () {
        // collect relevant product sku and name
        wsSliderProductSku = $(this).parent().parent().find('.product-tile__quickview-trigger').attr('data-product-sku');
        wsSliderProductName = $(this).parent().parent().parent().find('h3').text();

        // console.log product sku and name
        console.log("image click product code: " + wsSliderProductSku);
        console.log("image click product name: " + wsSliderProductName);

        dataLayer.push({
            'event': 'hp_product_carousel',
            'eventaction' : 'Product Image clicked', 
            'eventcategory' : 'HP Product Carousel',
            'eventlabel' : wsSliderProductSku + " " + wsSliderProductName  
        }); 
    });

	"use strict";
	// add the title attribute to each .cta link throughout 
	$('section a.cta').each(function () {
		var $thisText = $(this).text().trim();
		$(this).attr('title', $thisText);
	});

	$('section .pagination').each(function () {
		//homepage and sale page only and add title attribute
		if ($(this).parents('body').hasClass('homepage') || $(this).parents('body').hasClass('sales-landing-page')) {
			$(this).children('li').children('a').each(function () {
				if ($(this).parent('li').hasClass('gender')) {
					var $thisText = $(this).text().toLowerCase().trim();
					$(this).attr('title', $thisText);
				} else {
					var $parentText = $(this).parents('ul').children('li:first-of-type').text();
					var $thisText = $(this).text().toLowerCase().trim();
					$(this).attr('title', $parentText + ' size ' + $thisText);
				}
			});

		} else {
			// add the title attribute to landong pages
			var $sectioncta = $(this).parents('section').find('a.cta').text();
				$(this).children('li').children('a').each(function () {
				var $thisText = $(this).text().toLowerCase().trim();
				$(this).attr('title', $sectioncta + ' size ' + $thisText);
			});
		}
	});

var elem = function (id) {
return document.getElementById(id);
};
var prefixTimeStr = function (str) {
if (str < 10) {
return '0' + str;
}
else {
return str;
}
}

var serverOffsetInSeconds = 3600;
var localOffsetInSeconds = new Date().getTimezoneOffset() * 60; // Seconds
var offsetDelta = (-serverOffsetInSeconds - localOffsetInSeconds) * 1000; // Milliseconds
var timerInit = false;
var timerElem = elem("countdown"),
daysElem = elem("countdown-days"),
hoursElem = elem("countdown-hours"),
minutesElem = elem("countdown-mins"),
secondsElem = elem("countdown-secs"),
prevDays = prevHours = prevMins = null,
countDownDate = new Date(2019, 10-1, 27, 23, 59, 59).getTime() + offsetDelta;
// Update the count down every 1 second
var countDownInterval = setInterval(function () {
// Initialise - fade in
if (!timerInit) {
timerElem.className += " countdown--init";
timerInit = true;
}
// Get todays date and time, find the difference between now and the date
var now = new Date().getTime();
var distance = countDownDate - now;
// Time calculations for days, hours, minutes and seconds
var days = prefixTimeStr(Math.floor(distance / (1000 * 60 * 60 * 24)));
var hours = prefixTimeStr(Math.floor((distance % (1000 * 60 * 60 * 24)) / (1000 * 60 * 60)));
var minutes = prefixTimeStr(Math.floor((distance % (1000 * 60 * 60)) / (1000 * 60)));
var seconds = prefixTimeStr(Math.floor((distance % (1000 * 60)) / 1000));
if (distance < 0) {
// If the count down is finished, set 00 to all elements (will probably never see this)
daysElem.innerHTML = "00";
hoursElem.innerHTML = "00";
minutesElem.innerHTML = "00";
secondsElem.innerHTML = "00";
clearInterval(countDownInterval);
} else {
// To prevent updating all elements every second, check if they are different
if (days != prevDays) {
daysElem.innerHTML = days;
prevDays = days;
// If 0 days left, hide the days element
if (parseInt(days) < 1) {
timerElem.className += " countdown--nodays"
}
}
if (hours != prevHours) {
hoursElem.innerHTML = hours;
prevHours = hours;
}
if (minutes != prevMins) {
minutesElem.innerHTML = minutes;
prevMins = minutes;
}
secondsElem.innerHTML = seconds;
}
}, 1000);
</script>
